# Coverage table: Telecommunications Act 1999 (2020 Revised Edition deposit) and the instruments deposited under it

Generated by `gen-coverage.py` from the deposited texts and from the `@ref` locators of the rule modules; do not edit by hand.

Dispositions: `encoded` (every content line of the row is quoted and cited by a rule: see the module column), `inert` (reason 1 not operative, 2 machinery, 3 repealed or blank), `fork` (a fork-register entry; the lines are also quoted by the rule for the chosen reading), `pending` (working state; the final table has none).

`2000 Ed. s` is the number the Law Revision Commission's margin marker gives the section in the 2000 Revised Edition; the pre-2020 instruments cite those numbers.

## Instruments made under the Act: one row per provision of each deposited text

Source of the list: IMDA's Telecommunications Act 1999 page (https://www.imda.gov.sg/regulations-and-licensing-listing/telecommunications-act-1999), fetched 2026-11-03: 40 PDFs, each deposited with its text under `subjects/sg/telecommunications-*/registers/source-bundle/`.
The page lists nothing after S 100/2016 (Rg 5), S 726/2014 (N 1) or S 725/2014 (Rg 6); whether the list is current is not established.
A provision's `lines` are lines of its own text file. An amending instrument's row is the provision of the amending instrument (its own words), not the amended text.
The instruments under the Broadcasting Act are in the Broadcasting Act folder's own table.
